   557	/// Lazy lanes need a position that identifies one row within the lane.
   558	/// Keep setup evaluation and non-uniform traversal in their original order.
   559	pub fn lazy_merge(schema: &Schema, merge: &Merge) -> bool {
   560	    let Some(first) = merge.lanes.first() else { return true };
   561	    if merge.branches() { return branch_merge_lazy(schema, merge); }
   562	    merge.lanes.iter().all(|lane| {
   563	        let scan = &lane.scan;
   564	        lane.project.is_none() && lane.setup.is_empty() && scan.table == first.scan.table && scan.index == first.scan.index
   565	            && scan.prefix.iter().enumerate().all(|(position, key)| matches!(key, IndexKeyExpr::Scalar(_)) || position + 1 == scan.prefix.len())
   566	            && scan.order == first.scan.order && scan.prefix.len() == first.scan.prefix.len()
   567	            && scan_visibility(schema, scan).uniform
   568	            && schema.table(&scan.table).is_some_and(|table| table.index(&scan.index).is_some_and(|index| {
   569	                // Nullable unique keys allow multiple nulls; they are not a
   570	                // position from which a one-row refill can safely resume.
   571	                let total_unique = index.unique && index.components.iter().all(|component| component.key_type(table).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()));
   572	                scan.prefix.len() < index.components.len() && (total_unique || index.columns().last().is_some_and(|c| c == "id"))
   573	            }))
   574	    })
   575	}
   576	
   577	/// A branch merge's lanes may read different indexes of one table: each
   578	/// resumes on its own index from the shared merge position, so every lane
   579	/// needs scalar pins, a total position and the same position columns. A
   580	/// party or `where` lane must be decided by its prefix; a grant lane is
   581	/// decided per caller before it opens (`word_merge.rs`).
   582	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#3 — the binding check makes these hold; a lane that fails one keeps the eager traversal.
   583	pub fn branch_merge_lazy(schema: &Schema, merge: &Merge) -> bool {
   584	    let Some(first) = merge.lanes.first() else { return true };
   585	    let Some(table) = schema.table(&first.scan.table) else { return false };
   586	    let mut suffix: Option<(&crate::schema::Index, usize)> = None;
   587	    merge.lanes.iter().all(|lane| {
   588	        let scan = &lane.scan;
   589	        let Some(index) = table.index(&scan.index) else { return false };
   590	        let plain = index.components.iter().all(|component| component.column().is_some());
   591	        let total_unique = index.unique && plain && index.components.iter().all(|component| component.column()
   592	            .and_then(|column| table.columns.get(column)).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()));
   593	        let prefix_total_unique = table.indexes.values().filter(|candidate| candidate.unique && candidate.is_plain())
   594	            .filter(|candidate| candidate.components.iter().all(|component| component.column()
   595	                .and_then(|column| table.columns.get(column)).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional())))
   596	            .any(|candidate| candidate.components.iter().all(|part| part.column().is_some_and(|column|
   597	                index.components.iter().take(scan.prefix.len()).any(|prefix| prefix.column() == Some(column)))));
   598	        // A membership target selected by its intrinsic id is physically a
   599	        // fully pinned byId point, but its logical merge position is that id.
   600	        // Compare it as the unpinned id suffix used by every other lane.
   601	        let position_prefix = scan.prefix.len() - usize::from(membership_point_id(schema, lane));
   602	        let same = match suffix {
   603	            Some((first_index, first_prefix)) => same_merge_position(table, first_index, first_prefix, index, position_prefix),
   604	            None => { suffix = Some((index, position_prefix)); true }
   605	        };
   606	        let authority = if lane.membership() { membership_branch(schema, lane) }
   607	            else if lane.system() { lane.source.is_none() }
   608	            else if lane.vacancy() { vacancy_branch(schema, lane) }
   609	            else if lane.grant() { lane.source.is_none() && covering_grant(schema, scan).is_some() }
   610	            else if lane.guarded() { lane.source.is_none() && page_guard_decision(schema, scan).is_some() }
   611	            else { lane.source.is_none() && scan_visibility(schema, scan).uniform };
   612	        lane.project.is_none() && lane.setup.is_empty() && scan.table == first.scan.table
   613	            && scan.prefix.iter().all(|key| matches!(key, IndexKeyExpr::Scalar(_)))
   614	            && scan.order == first.scan.order && !scan.existence && same
   615	            && (scan.prefix.len() < index.components.len() || prefix_total_unique || membership_point_id(schema, lane))
   616	            && index.word_component().is_none()
   617	            // A dynamic target-id lane uses `[id, order…]`: its scalar id
   618	            // prefix is already a total point even though the physical index
   619	            // is non-unique and need not repeat id as a suffix.
   620	            && plain && (total_unique || prefix_total_unique
   621	                || index.components.last().and_then(|component| component.column()).is_some_and(|c| c == "id"))
   622	            && authority
   623	    })
   624	}
   625	
   626	/// Exhaustion is observable only when the authored read rule itself contains
   627	/// a top-level absence check on an exact required point keyed by this row's
   628	/// intrinsic id. The lane stays private and evaluates the complete rule.
   629	fn vacancy_branch(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
   630	    if lane.source.is_some() || lane.scan.filter.is_some() || !lane.scan.prefix.is_empty() { return false; }
   631	    let Some(outer) = schema.table(&lane.scan.table) else { return false };
   632	    if outer.sync.is_some() { return false; }
   633	    outer.rules.read.disjuncts().iter().any(|rule| {
   634	        let RuleExpr::Not(inner) = rule else { return false };
   635	        let RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. } = inner.as_ref() else { return false };
   636	        let Some(target) = schema.table(table) else { return false };
   637	        let Some(point) = target.index(index) else { return false };
   638	        equalities.is_empty() && key == &[RuleTerm::Field("id".into())]
   639	            && point.unique && point.is_plain() && point.components.len() == 1
   640	            && point.components[0].column().is_some_and(|column|
   641	                target.columns.get(column).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()))
   642	    })
   643	}
   644	
   645	/// An intrinsic-id membership lane retains id as its logical merge position
   646	/// even though the physical byId scan pins that sole component.
   647	pub fn membership_point_id(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
   648	    if !lane.membership() || lane.scan.range.is_some() { return false; }
   649	    let Some(table) = schema.table(&lane.scan.table) else { return false };
   650	    let Some(index) = table.index(&lane.scan.index) else { return false };
   651	    let Some(Expr::Field { base, name: source_column }) = lane.distinct.as_ref() else { return false };
   652	    if !matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == &lane.binding) { return false; }
   653	    lane.scan.prefix.len() == index.components.len()
   654	        && index.components.last().and_then(|part| part.column()).is_some_and(|column| column == "id")
   655	        && membership_outer_column(schema, lane, source_column) == Some("id")
   656	}
   657	
   658	/// The source is readable under its fixed pins, and its row is the exact
   659	/// witness for the outer rule branch that the dynamic prefix opens. A cap on
   660	/// the fixed source group is one admission proof; a total point witness is
   661	/// another. The latter bounds each witness, while the shared read meter safely
   662	/// cuts the otherwise-unbounded inverted discovery before any row is emitted.
   663	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#15 — membership discovery proves each parent range without treating point uniqueness as a global membership cap.
   664	fn membership_branch(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
   665	    membership_witness(schema, lane, false)
   666	}
   667	
   668	/// A null discovered value may open a target lane only when the matching
   669	/// point witness identifies its row without that nullable component. A by-id
   670	/// probe with `parent = null` does; a nullable natural unique does not.
   671	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#14 — fixed-id witnesses preserve nullable residual equality without treating nullable natural keys as points.
   672	pub(crate) fn membership_null_distinct(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
   673	    membership_witness(schema, lane, true)
   674	}
   675	
   676	/// Whether PAGE v1's validator would admit this already-valid membership
   677	/// lane. Capability derivation uses the historical predicate exactly, so
   678	/// capped §14 plans remain v1-compatible while every newly admitted shape is
   679	/// fenced by `branch-membership-v2`.
   680	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#15 — PAGE2 capability negotiation preserves the v1 admission boundary.
   681	pub fn membership_v1(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
   682	    if !lane.membership() { return false; }
   683	    let Some(source) = lane.source.as_ref().and_then(crate::ir::LaneSource::scan) else { return false };
   684	    let Some(Expr::Field { base, name: source_column }) = lane.distinct.as_ref() else { return false };
   685	    if !matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(name) if name == &lane.binding) { return false; }
   686	    let Some(source_table) = schema.table(&source.table) else { return false };
   687	    let Some(source_index) = source_table.index(&source.index) else { return false };
   688	    let next_component = source.prefix.len() < source_index.components.len()
   689	        && source_index.components.get(source.prefix.len()).and_then(|part| part.column()) == Some(source_column);
   690	    let one_row_source = source.prefix.len() == source_index.components.len()
   691	        && source_index.unique && source_index.is_plain()
   692	        && source_index.components.iter().all(|part| part.column().and_then(|column| source_table.columns.get(column))
   693	            .is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()));
   694	    if source.existence || (!next_component && !one_row_source) || !scan_visibility(schema, source).decided_true { return false; }
   695	    let Bound::Literal(take @ 1..) = source.take else { return false };
   696	    let prefix_has = |field: &String| source_index.components.iter().take(source.prefix.len())
   697	        .any(|part| part.column() == Some(field));
   698	    let cap = source_table.constraints.iter().filter_map(|constraint| match constraint {
   699	        crate::schema::Constraint::Cap { by, max } if by.iter().all(prefix_has) && !source_table.cap_exempts_null(by) => Some(*max),
   700	        _ => None,
   701	    }).min();
   702	    if cap.is_none_or(|cap| cap > take && !one_row_source) { return false; }
   703	    membership_outer_column(schema, lane, source_column).is_some_and(|column| column != "id")
   704	}
   705	
   706	fn membership_outer_column<'a>(schema: &'a Schema, lane: &'a crate::ir::MergeLane, source_column: &str) -> Option<&'a str> {
   707	    let table = schema.table(&lane.scan.table)?;
   708	    let index = table.index(&lane.scan.index)?;
   709	    let mut outer = None;
   710	    for (part, key) in index.components.iter().zip(&lane.scan.prefix) {
   711	        let (Some(column), Some(expr)) = (part.column(), key.scalar()) else { return None };
   712	        if matches!(expr, Expr::Field { base, name } if matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == &lane.binding) && name == source_column)
   713	            && outer.replace(column).is_some() { return None; }
   714	    }
   715	    outer.map(String::as_str)
   716	}
   717	
   718	fn membership_witness(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane, null_distinct: bool) -> bool {
   719	    let Some(source) = lane.source.as_ref().and_then(crate::ir::LaneSource::scan) else { return false };
   720	    let Some(Expr::Field { base, name: source_column }) = lane.distinct.as_ref() else { return false };
   721	    if !matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(name) if name == &lane.binding) { return false; }
   722	    let Some(source_table) = schema.table(&source.table) else { return false };
   723	    let Some(source_index) = source_table.index(&source.index) else { return false };
   724	    let next_component = source.prefix.len() < source_index.components.len()
   725	        && source_index.components.get(source.prefix.len()).and_then(|part| part.column()) == Some(source_column);
   726	    // A fully pinned required unique identifies at most one source row, so
   727	    // any of that row's columns can be the discovered value. The source need
   728	    // not walk the distinct column as a following index component.
   729	    let one_row_source = source.prefix.len() == source_index.components.len()
   730	        && source_index.unique && source_index.is_plain()
   731	        && source_index.components.iter().all(|part| part.column().and_then(|column| source_table.columns.get(column))
   732	            .is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()));
   733	    if source.existence || (!next_component && !one_row_source)
   734	        || !scan_visibility(schema, source).decided_true { return false; }
   735	    // An applicable declaration can compile to a literal source bound. An
   736	    // exact total point may instead use the ordinary read meter: discovery
   737	    // remains fenced until that metered scan completes.
   738	    if !matches!(source.take, Bound::Meter | Bound::Literal(1..)) { return false; }
   739	    let prefix_has = |field: &String| source_index.components.iter().take(source.prefix.len())
   740	        .any(|part| part.column() == Some(field));
   741	    let capped_source_group = source_table.constraints.iter().any(|constraint| match constraint {
   742	        crate::schema::Constraint::Cap { by, max } if by.iter().all(prefix_has) && !source_table.cap_exempts_null(by) => Some(*max),
   743	        _ => None,
   744	    }.is_some());
   745	    let Some(outer_table) = schema.table(&lane.scan.table) else { return false };
   746	    let Some(outer_index) = outer_table.index(&lane.scan.index) else { return false };
   747	    let mut outer_column: Option<&String> = None;
   748	    for (part, key) in outer_index.components.iter().zip(&lane.scan.prefix) {
   749	        let (Some(column), Some(expr)) = (part.column(), key.scalar()) else { return false };
   750	        if matches!(expr, Expr::Field { base, name } if matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == &lane.binding) && name == source_column)
   751	            && outer_column.replace(column).is_some() { return false; }
   752	    }
   753	    let Some(outer_column) = outer_column else { return false };
   754	    let fixed = |column: &String| source_index.components.iter().zip(&source.prefix)
   755	        .find_map(|(part, key)| (part.column() == Some(column)).then(|| key.scalar()).flatten())
   756	        .or_else(|| source.filter.as_deref().and_then(|filter| required_source_equality(filter, column)));
   757	    let pins = scan_pinned(outer_table, &lane.scan);
   758	    let pinned = |column: &str| pins.iter().find_map(|(name, value)| (name.as_str() == column).then_some(*value));
   759	    membership_rule(&outer_table.rules.read, &|rule| {
   760	        let RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. } = rule else { return false };
   761	        let Some(probe_index) = source_table.index(index) else { return false };
   762	        if table != &source.table || !probe_index.unique || !probe_index.is_plain() || probe_index.components.len() != key.len() { return false; }
   763	        let total_point = probe_index.components.iter().all(|part| part.column()
   764	            .and_then(|column| source_table.columns.get(column)).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()));
   765	        if !one_row_source && !capped_source_group && !total_point { return false; }
   766	        let matches = |column: &String, term: &RuleTerm| if column == source_column { matches!(term, RuleTerm::Field(field) if field == outer_column) }
   767	            else { fixed(column).is_some_and(|expr| match term {
   768	                // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#17 — an exact target pin fixes one component of a multi-column point witness.
   769	                RuleTerm::Field(field) => pinned(field).is_some_and(|pin| pin == expr),
   770	                _ => term_matches_expr(term, expr),
   771	            }) };
   772	        if !probe_index.components.iter().zip(key).all(|(part, term)| part.column().is_some_and(|column| matches(column, term)))
   773	            || !equalities.iter().all(|(column, term)| matches(column, term)) { return false; }
   774	        !null_distinct || probe_index.components.iter().all(|part| part.column().is_some_and(|column| column != source_column
   775	            && fixed(column).is_some_and(|expr| matches!(expr, Expr::Input(Input::Viewer)) || fixed_non_null_literal(expr))))
   776	    }, &|rule| rule_true(rule, &lane.scan.table, outer_table, &pinned)
   777	        || lane.scan.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(|filter|
   778	            fixed_rule_holds(schema, filter, rule, &lane.scan.table, outer_table, &pinned, &pins)))
   779	}
   780	
   781	/// Every sibling of a varying membership witness must already hold. Preserve
   782	/// authored Boolean grouping: every fixed conjunct is required, while proving
   783	/// one fixed alternative is enough for an authored disjunction.
   784	fn fixed_rule_holds<'a>(schema: &Schema, filter: &Expr, rule: &RuleExpr,
   785	    outer_name: &str, outer_table: &Table, pinned: &impl Fn(&str) -> Option<&'a Expr>,
   786	    pins: &[(&String, &Expr)]) -> bool {
   787	    if rule_true(rule, outer_name, outer_table, pinned) { return true; }
   788	    match rule {
   789	        RuleExpr::And(parts) => parts.iter().all(|part|
   790	            fixed_rule_holds(schema, filter, part, outer_name, outer_table, pinned, pins)),
   791	        RuleExpr::Or(parts) => parts.iter().any(|part|
   792	            fixed_rule_holds(schema, filter, part, outer_name, outer_table, pinned, pins)),
   793	        _ => fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, filter, rule, pins),
   794	    }
   795	}
   796	
   797	/// A generated membership lane may retain prefix-fixed point grants
   798	/// as a residual predicate.  Recognize only the exact bound point expression:
   799	/// this proves that every row emitted by the lane satisfies that conjunct,
   800	/// while a missing, reordered or merely similar probe keeps the merge out.
   801	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — fixed grants may guard one varying per-row grant.
   802	fn fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema: &Schema, filter: &Expr, rule: &RuleExpr,
   803	    pins: &[(&String, &Expr)]) -> bool {
   804	    if let Expr::Binary { op: crate::ir::BinaryOp::And, left, right } = filter {
   805	        return fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, left, rule, pins)
   806	            || fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, right, rule, pins);
   807	    }
   808	    // The IR preserves short-circuit `a and b` as a tiny Boolean block.
   809	    // Accept only that exact lowering: both operands are necessary, while an
   810	    // `or` block assigns on the false branch and cannot establish either one.
   811	    if let Expr::Block { body, value } = filter
   812	        && let [crate::ir::Stmt::Let { name, value: left }, crate::ir::Stmt::If {
   813	            condition: Expr::Variable(condition), then_body, else_body }] = body.as_slice()
   814	        && let [crate::ir::Stmt::Assign { name: assigned, value: right }] = then_body.as_slice()
   815	        && else_body.is_empty() && condition == name && assigned == name
   816	        && matches!(value.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(result) if result == name) {
   817	        return fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, left, rule, pins)
   818	            || fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, right, rule, pins);
   819	    }
   820	    let (Expr::Exists(read), RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. }) = (filter, rule) else { return false };
   821	    let Some(target) = schema.table(table) else { return false };
   822	    let Some(point) = target.index(index) else { return false };
   823	    equalities.is_empty() && target.through.is_none() && target.not_before.is_none() && !target.is_expiring()
   824	        && point.unique && point.is_plain() && point.components.iter().all(|part| part.column()
   825	            .and_then(|column| target.columns.get(column)).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()))
   826	        && &read.table == table && &read.index == index && read.key.len() == key.len()
   827	        && read.key.iter().zip(key).all(|(expression, term)| rule_term_expression(term, expression, pins))
   828	        && point_read_is_visible(schema, read)
   829	}
   830	
   831	/// A rule probe uses system authority, but the retained residual point uses
   832	/// ordinary caller authority. Re-prove that the generated, pin-substituted
   833	/// point is visible instead of asking about the unsubstituted authored terms.
   834	fn point_read_is_visible(schema: &Schema, read: &crate::ir::Read) -> bool {
   835	    let Some(table) = schema.table(&read.table) else { return false };
   836	    let Some(index) = table.index(&read.index) else { return false };
   837	    if !index.is_plain() || index.components.len() != read.key.len() { return false; }
   838	    let pinned = |name: &str| index.components.iter().zip(&read.key).find_map(|(part, value)|
   839	        (part.column().is_some_and(|column| column == name)).then_some(value));
   840	    visibility(&read.table, table, &table.rules.read, &pinned).decided_true
   841	}
   842	
   843	fn rule_term_expression(term: &RuleTerm, expression: &Expr, pins: &[(&String, &Expr)]) -> bool {
   844	    match (term, expression) {
   845	        (RuleTerm::Viewer, Expr::Input(Input::Viewer)) => true,
   846	        (RuleTerm::Literal(value), Expr::Literal(actual)) => value == actual,
   847	        (RuleTerm::Field(field), expression) => pins.iter().find_map(|(name, value)|
   848	            (name.as_str() == field).then_some(*value)).is_some_and(|value| value == expression),
   849	        _ => false,
   850	    }
   851	}
   852	
   853	/// A witness supplies one conjunct; every other conjunct must already hold
   854	/// by the lane's pins. Distribution does not drop a status/role condition.
   855	fn membership_rule(rule: &RuleExpr, witness: &impl Fn(&RuleExpr) -> bool, pinned: &impl Fn(&RuleExpr) -> bool) -> bool {
   856	    match rule {
   857	        RuleExpr::Or(parts) => parts.iter().any(|part| membership_rule(part, witness, pinned)),
   858	        RuleExpr::And(parts) => parts.iter().enumerate().any(|(at, part)|
   859	            membership_rule(part, witness, pinned) && parts.iter().enumerate().all(|(other, rule)| at == other || pinned(rule))),
   860	        _ => witness(rule),
   861	    }
   862	}
   863	
   864	/// A generated membership discovery filter may retain an exact fixed probe
   865	/// component after its capped prefix (`id = 'm1'`, for example). Only a bare
   866	/// equality in the filter's conjunction is a necessary fact about every
   867	/// discovered row; alternatives and other implications remain unsupported.
   868	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#14 — fixed membership witness columns may be proved by exact source residuals.
   869	fn required_source_equality<'a>(filter: &'a Expr, column: &str) -> Option<&'a Expr> {
   870	    match filter {
   871	        Expr::Binary { op: crate::ir::BinaryOp::And, left, right } =>
   872	            required_source_equality(left, column).or_else(|| required_source_equality(right, column)),
   873	        Expr::Binary { op: crate::ir::BinaryOp::Eq, left, right } if source_field(left, column) => Some(right),
   874	        Expr::Binary { op: crate::ir::BinaryOp::Eq, left, right } if source_field(right, column) => Some(left),
   875	        _ => None,
   876	    }
   877	}
   878	
   879	fn source_field(expr: &Expr, column: &str) -> bool {
   880	    matches!(expr, Expr::Field { base, name }
   881	        if name == column && matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == "$filter"))
   882	}
   883	
   884	fn term_matches_expr(term: &RuleTerm, expr: &Expr) -> bool {
   885	    match (term, expr) { (RuleTerm::Viewer, Expr::Input(Input::Viewer)) => true,
   886	        (RuleTerm::Literal(value), expr) => literal_matches_expr(value, expr), _ => false }
   887	}
   888	
   889	fn literal_matches_expr(value: &Value, expr: &Expr) -> bool {
   890	    match expr {
   891	        Expr::Literal(other) => value == other,
   892	        Expr::Unary { op: crate::ir::UnaryOp::Neg, .. } => matches!(value, Value::Int(expected)
   893	            if expr_int_literal(expr).is_some_and(|actual| actual == i128::from(*expected))),
   894	        _ => false,
   895	    }
   896	}
   897	
   898	fn expr_int_literal(expr: &Expr) -> Option<i128> {
   899	    match expr {
   900	        Expr::Literal(Value::Int(value)) => Some(i128::from(*value)),
   901	        Expr::Unary { op: crate::ir::UnaryOp::Neg, value } => expr_int_literal(value).map(|value| -value),
   902	        _ => None,
   903	    }
   904	}

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	//! Bounded positive DNF of a read rule. Each conjunct is retained in its
     2	//! range; a guard opens only that range, never the other rule branches.
     3	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#16 — complete compound pages use the same bounded merge and discovery fence.
     4	use super::{catalog::TableInfo, intent::{self, Requests}, intent_branches as branches};
     5	use crate::ast::*;
     6	use snapback4_core::ir::{BRANCH_LANE, GUARDED_LANE, SYSTEM_LANE, VACANCY_LANE};
     7	use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
     8	
     9	type Tables = BTreeMap<String, TableInfo>;
    10	
    11	/// Comparisons and readable static points have a total logical predicate.
    12	/// A physical point can still cut: the merge frontier excludes an unfinished
    13	/// filter candidate, so continuation retries it rather than skipping it.
    14	pub(super) fn total_readable(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &BTreeSet<(String, String)>, focal: &TableInfo, value: &Expr) -> bool {
    15	    if branches::total(value) { return true; }
    16	    match value {
    17	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "and" || op == "or" =>
    18	            total_readable(tables, synthesized, focal, left) && total_readable(tables, synthesized, focal, right),
    19	        Expr::Exists { read: probe, .. } if branches::columns_within(value, &[]) => {
    20	            if probe.range.is_some() || probe.filter.is_some() || probe.matching.is_some() || !probe.seeks.is_empty() { return false; }
    21	            if !probe.keys.iter().all(|key| fixed_key_total(&key.value)) { return false; }
    22	            let Some(target) = tables.get(&probe.table) else { return false };
    23	            if target.through.is_some() || target.decl.not_before.is_some() || target.decl.expire.is_some() { return false; }
    24	            let Some(columns) = super::intent_lanes::probe_columns(tables, synthesized, focal, probe) else { return false };
    25	            if !target.indexes.iter().any(|index| target.total_unique(index) && index.columns.iter().all(|column| columns.contains(column))) { return false; }
    26	            let mut point = probe.clone();
    27	            for (key, column) in point.keys.iter_mut().zip(columns) { key.column = Some(column); }
    28	            target.decl.public.is_some() || branches::read_rule(target).is_some_and(|source_rule|
    29	                branches::by_form(source_rule, &point) || crate::print::expression(source_rule) == crate::print::expression(value))
    30	        }
    31	        _ => false,
    32	    }
    33	}
    34	
    35	/// Distribution must not evaluate a fallible key on an overlapping party's
    36	/// row where the original short circuit skipped it. Signed numeric literals
    37	/// are bound constants; arithmetic, assertions, calls and reads stay put.
    38	fn fixed_key_total(value: &Expr) -> bool {
    39	    matches!(value, Expr::Name { .. } | Expr::Literal { .. })
    40	        || matches!(value, Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "-" && matches!(value.as_ref(), Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Number(_), .. }))
    41	}
    42	
    43	pub(super) fn expand(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &BTreeSet<(String, String)>, requests: &mut Requests, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<MergeExpr> {
    44	    let range = read.range.as_ref()?;
    45	    let table = tables.get(&read.table)?;
    46	    if read.index.is_some() || !read.keys.is_empty() || read.matching.is_some() || !read.computed.is_empty() || read.cursor_prefix.is_some()
    47	        || table.maintained.is_some() || table.through.is_some() || table.decl.ephemeral.is_some() || !table.captures.is_empty() || table.decl.public.is_some()
    48	        || read.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(|filter| !branches::total(filter)) { return None; }
    49	    let rule = branches::read_rule(table)?;
    50	    if let Some(merge) = vacancy(tables, synthesized, requests, read) { return Some(merge); }
    51	    // Keep the published simple plans and descriptors. This pass handles
    52	    // compound rules, not the existing party/grant or exact membership rule.
    53	    if !compound(rule) && !matches!(rule, Expr::Binary { op, .. } if op == "or") { return None; }
    54	    let leaves = dnf(table, rule)?;
    55	    if leaves.len() + 1 > branches::MAX_BRANCHES { return None; }
    56	    let mut candidate = requests.clone();
    57	    let errors = candidate.error_count();
    58	    let mut lanes = Vec::new();
    59	    let mut order = None;
    60	    let mut system = read.clone(); candidate.read(tables, &mut system, false);
    61	    if branches::decided(tables, &system) { return None; }

    69	            } else if intent::equality(atom, table).is_some() {
    70	                pins.push(atom.clone());
    71	            } else if matches!(atom, Expr::Unary { .. }) && !branches::columns_within(atom, &[]) {

   115	                key.column.as_ref().filter(|_| fixed_key_total(&key.value)).map(|column| (column.clone(), key.value.clone()))).collect();

   159	
   160	/// A top-level `not exists point[.id]` explicitly makes vacancy observable.
   161	/// One server lane checks the original rule for every candidate and may report
   162	/// exhaustion only after the physical range ends. It carries no ordinary
   163	/// filter because rule probes use wider system visibility.
   164	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — exact vacancy is the sole non-uniform exhaustion lane.
   165	pub(super) fn vacancy(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &BTreeSet<(String, String)>, requests: &mut Requests,
   166	    read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<MergeExpr> {
   167	    let range = read.range.as_ref()?;
   168	    let table = tables.get(&read.table)?;
   169	    let rule = branches::read_rule(table)?;
   170	    if read.filter.is_some() || read.index.is_some() || !read.keys.is_empty() || read.matching.is_some()
   171	        || !read.computed.is_empty() || read.cursor_prefix.is_some()
   172	        || (table.decl.sync.is_some() && !table.decl.online_only) { return None; }
   173	    fn alternatives<'a>(value: &'a Expr, out: &mut Vec<&'a Expr>) {
   174	        match value {
   175	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => { alternatives(left, out); alternatives(right, out); }
   176	            other => out.push(other),
   177	        }
   178	    }
   179	    let mut disjuncts = Vec::new(); alternatives(rule, &mut disjuncts);
   180	    let admitted = disjuncts.iter().any(|value| {
   181	        let Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } = value else { return false };
   182	        let Expr::Exists { read: probe, .. } = value.as_ref() else { return false };
   183	        if op != "not" || probe.range.is_some() || probe.filter.is_some() || probe.matching.is_some()
   184	            || !probe.seeks.is_empty() || probe.keys.len() != 1 || intent::column(&probe.keys[0].value) != Some("id") { return false; }
   185	        let Some(target) = tables.get(&probe.table) else { return false };
   186	        let Some(columns) = super::intent_lanes::probe_columns(tables, synthesized, table, probe) else { return false };
   187	        target.indexes.iter().any(|index| target.total_unique(index) && index.columns == columns)
   188	    });
   189	    if !admitted { return None; }
   190	    let mut candidate = requests.clone(); let errors = candidate.error_count();
   191	    let mut system = read.clone(); candidate.read(tables, &mut system, false);
   192	    let mut lane = read.clone(); candidate.plan_lane(tables, &mut lane);
   193	    let mut lanes = Vec::with_capacity(2); let mut order = None;
   194	    branches::push_membership_lane(&candidate, table, &mut lanes, &mut order, system, SYSTEM_LANE, None, None, None)?;
   195	    branches::push_membership_lane(&candidate, table, &mut lanes, &mut order, lane, VACANCY_LANE, None, None, None)?;
   196	    if candidate.error_count() != errors { return None; }
   197	    *requests = candidate;
   198	    Some(MergeExpr { view_order: Vec::new(), table: read.table.clone(), matching: None, filter: None,
   199	        index: String::new(), order: range.order, take: range.take, cursor: range.cursor.clone(), lanes, site: read.site.clone() })
   200	}
   201	
   202	fn compound(rule: &Expr) -> bool {
   203	    match rule {
   204	        Expr::Binary { op, .. } if op == "and" || op == "!=" => true,
   205	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => compound(left) || compound(right),
   206	        _ => false,
   207	    }
   208	}
   209	
   210	/// At most seven positive alternatives plus the system range. A nullable
   211	/// enum's `!= null` is exactly its finite set of non-null values.
   212	fn dnf(table: &TableInfo, value: &Expr) -> Option<Vec<Vec<Expr>>> {
   213	    let out = match value {
   214	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => {
   215	            let mut out = dnf(table, left)?; out.extend(dnf(table, right)?); out
   216	        }
   217	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "and" => {
   218	            let a = dnf(table, left)?; let b = dnf(table, right)?;
   219	            if a.len().saturating_mul(b.len()) >= branches::MAX_BRANCHES { return None; }
   220	            a.into_iter().flat_map(|a| b.iter().map(move |b| { let mut out = a.clone(); out.extend(b.clone()); out })).collect()
   221	        }
   222	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, site } if op == "!=" => {
   223	            let (column, term) = if let Some(column) = intent::column(left) { (column, right) } else { (intent::column(right)?, left) };
   224	            let kind = table.columns.get(column)?;
   225	            let TypeKind::Enum { variants } = &kind.kind else { return None; };
   226	            let include = |variant: &String| match term.as_ref() {
   227	                Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Null, .. } if kind.optional => Some(true),
   228	                Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Str(excluded), .. } if !kind.optional => Some(variant != excluded),
   229	                _ => None,
   230	            };
   231	            if variants.iter().any(|variant| include(variant).is_none()) { return None; }
   232	            variants.iter().filter(|variant| include(variant).unwrap()).map(|variant| vec![Expr::Binary { op: "=".into(), left: Box::new(Expr::Member {
   233	                base: Box::new(Expr::Focal { site: site.clone() }), name: column.into(), site: site.clone() }),
   234	                right: Box::new(Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Str(variant.clone()), site: site.clone() }), site: site.clone() }]).collect()
   235	        }
   236	        other => vec![vec![other.clone()]],
   237	    };
   238	    (out.len() < branches::MAX_BRANCHES).then_some(out)
   239	}

   556	/-- A page guard (LLP 3000.036 §12.1, §16.3): a disjunct whose expression

   646	                && (membershipColumns key).any fun pair =>
   647	                  !ps.any (·.1 == pair.2) && (!order.contains pair.2 || pair.2 == "id" && order == ["id"])

   657	/-- The exact positive where witness of PAGE4. Other audiences cannot
   658	contribute to W; its readable source may certify the narrower page. -/
   659	def Expr.isWhereWitness (r : Rule) (e : Expr) : Bool :=

   661	  | .probe rt _ rkeys, .bin .ne (.point et ekeys) (.lit (.s .null)) =>
   662	      rt == et && rkeys.length == ekeys.length && rkeys.all fun rk =>
   663	        ekeys.any fun ek => rk.1 == ek.1 && match rk.2, ek.2 with

   666	          | .lit a, .lit (.s b) => a == b
   667	          | _, _ => false
   668	  | _, _ => false

   670	def scopedWitnessDecided (sch : Schema) (td : TableDecl)
   671	    (pins : List (String × Expr)) (fs : List Expr) (order : List String) : Bool :=
   672	  td.read.disjuncts.any fun r => match r with
   673	    | .probe t point key =>

   677	            !pins.any (·.1 == pair.2)
   678	              && (!order.contains pair.2 || pair.2 == "id" && order == ["id"])

   697	  let fs := (filter.map Expr.conjuncts).getD []

   699	    p.1 != "id" && (order.head? == some p.1 || !order.contains p.1)

   701	/-- The branch rule of LLP 3000.036.001 §2 (cases 1 and 2; a grant branch is

   703	def branchDecided (sch : Schema) (t : String) (pins : List (String × Expr))
   704	    (filter : Option Expr) (order : List String) (guards : Bool := true) : Bool :=
   705	  match sch.table? t with
   706	  | none => false
   707	  | some td =>
   708	    let fs := (filter.map Expr.conjuncts).getD []
   709	    let total := pins.all (fun p => p.2.total) && fs.all (Expr.readablePredicate sch)
   710	    let readFree := pins.all (fun p => p.2.total) && fs.all Expr.total
   711	    let alts : Option (List (List Expr)) :=
   712	      match fs.filter Expr.isOr with
   713	      | [o] =>
   714	        let as := o.disjuncts
   715	        if 2 ≤ as.length && as.length ≤ 8 && as.all (fun a => a.conjuncts.any (eligiblePin td))
   716	        then some (as.map Expr.conjuncts) else none

   732	    -- case as well; otherwise case 1 could still classify the same read.

   735	      | .membership _ point key => if point == ["id"] then false else
   736	          (membershipColumns key).any fun pair =>
   737	            pins.any (·.1 == pair.2)
   738	              || alts.any fun bases => bases.any fun a => (a.filterMap Expr.eqPin).any (·.1 == pair.2)
   739	      | .party _ => false
   740	    let case1 := if reservedMembership || pinnedMembership then false else case1Candidate
   741	    let fixedMembership := td.read.accessLeaves.any fun leaves =>
   742	      let members := leaves.filter fun leaf => match leaf with | .membership .. => true | _ => false
   743	      !members.isEmpty && members.all fun leaf => match leaf with
   744	        | .membership source _ key => (membershipColumns key).any (fun pair => guardPins.any (·.1 == pair.2))
   745	            && (sch.table? source).any (fun source => source.notBefore.isNone)
   746	        | _ => false
   747	    let case2 := if !guards || !filterAlternativesSupported || ((reservedMembership || pinnedMembership) && !fixedMembership) then false else match td.read.branchParties sch td guardPins true with
   748	      | some ps =>
   749	        let bases := alts.getD [[]]
   750	        -- The grant lane is always planned (the system reads it whole).
   751	        ps.all (fun c => c != "id" && !order.contains c)
   752	          && bases.length * ps.length + 1 ≤ 8
   753	          && !(order.head?.any fun o => o != "id" && pins.any (·.1 == o))
   754	          && (guardPins.map (·.1)).eraseDups.length + order.length + 1 ≤ 8
   755	          && bases.all fun a => ps.all fun c => laneDecided td order ((c, .viewer) :: a.filterMap Expr.eqPin) pins
   756	      | none => false
   757	    -- PAGE: one system descriptor, party descriptors and eligible exact
   758	    -- membership descriptors. Dynamic discovery does not count against
   759	    -- eight; its static membership descriptor does.
   760	    let case3 := if !guards || !filterAlternativesSupported then false else match membershipRule with
   761	      | some leaves =>
   762	        let bases := alts.getD [[]]
   763	        let parties := leaves.filterMap fun leaf => match leaf with | .party c => some c | _ => none
   764	        let memberships := leaves.filter fun leaf => match leaf with | .membership _ _ _ => true | _ => false
   765	        bases.length * (parties.eraseDups.length + memberships.length) + 1 ≤ 8
   766	          && !(order.head?.any fun o => o != "id" && pins.any (·.1 == o))
   767	          && bases.all fun a => leaves.all fun leaf => match leaf with
   768	            | .party c => c != "id" && !order.contains c
   769	                && laneFits order ((c, .viewer) :: a.filterMap Expr.eqPin) pins
   770	            | .membership _ _ key => (membershipColumns key).any fun pair =>
   771	                (!order.contains pair.2 || pair.2 == "id" && order == ["id"])
   772	                  && !pins.any (·.1 == pair.2)
   773	                  && !(a.filterMap Expr.eqPin).any (·.1 == pair.2)
   774	                  && laneFits order ((pair.2, .viewer) :: a.filterMap Expr.eqPin) pins
   775	      | none => false
   776	    let case4 := guards && !reservedMembership && !pinnedMembership && alts.isNone && compoundDecided sch td pins order
   777	    let witnessScope := guards && pins.all (fun p => p.2.total) && scopedWitnessDecided sch td pins fs order
   778	    let vacancy := guards && pins.isEmpty && fs.isEmpty && vacancyDecided sch td
   779	    (total && (case1 || case2)) || (readFree && (case3 || case4)) || witnessScope || vacancy

   783	def parseCursor (order : List String) : Value → EM (Option Key)
   784	  | .s .null => pure none
   785	  | .arr xs =>
   786	    match xs.mapM (fun x => match x with | .s y => some y | _ => none) with
   787	    | some k => if k.length = order.length + 1 then pure (some k) else refuse "E_INPUT"
   788	    | none => refuse "E_INPUT"
   789	  | _ => refuse "E_INPUT"

   791	/-- A point read: the visible row with this id or unique key, or null; a
   792	null key component returns no row. -/
   793	def pointRead (vw : View) (t : String) (ks : List (String × Scalar)) : Value :=
   794	  if ks.any (·.2 == .null) then .null
   795	  else match (vw t).find? (keyMatch ks) with
   796	    | some r => r.toValue
   797	    | none => .null

   799	def markIncomplete : EM Unit := fun rs => .ok ((), { rs with complete := false })

   797	/// A generated membership lane may retain prefix-fixed point grants
   798	/// as a residual predicate.  Recognize only the exact bound point expression:
   799	/// this proves that every row emitted by the lane satisfies that conjunct,
   800	/// while a missing, reordered or merely similar probe keeps the merge out.
   801	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — fixed grants may guard one varying per-row grant.
   802	fn fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema: &Schema, filter: &Expr, rule: &RuleExpr,
   803	    pins: &[(&String, &Expr)]) -> bool {

   806	            || fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, right, rule, pins);

   808	    // The IR preserves short-circuit `a and b` as a tiny Boolean block.
   809	    // Accept only that exact lowering: both operands are necessary, while an
   810	    // `or` block assigns on the false branch and cannot establish either one.

   812	        && let [crate::ir::Stmt::Let { name, value: left }, crate::ir::Stmt::If {
   813	            condition: Expr::Variable(condition), then_body, else_body }] = body.as_slice()
   814	        && let [crate::ir::Stmt::Assign { name: assigned, value: right }] = then_body.as_slice()
   815	        && else_body.is_empty() && condition == name && assigned == name
   816	        && matches!(value.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(result) if result == name) {
   817	        return fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, left, rule, pins)
   818	            || fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, right, rule, pins);
   819	    }
   820	    let (Expr::Exists(read), RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. }) = (filter, rule) else { return false };
   821	    let Some(target) = schema.table(table) else { return false };
   822	    let Some(point) = target.index(index) else { return false };
   823	    equalities.is_empty() && target.through.is_none() && target.not_before.is_none() && !target.is_expiring()
   824	        && point.unique && point.is_plain() && point.components.iter().all(|part| part.column()
   825	            .and_then(|column| target.columns.get(column)).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()))
   826	        && &read.table == table && &read.index == index && read.key.len() == key.len()
   827	        && read.key.iter().zip(key).all(|(expression, term)| rule_term_expression(term, expression, pins))
   828	        && point_read_is_visible(schema, read)

   831	/// A rule probe uses system authority, but the retained residual point uses
   832	/// ordinary caller authority. Re-prove that the generated, pin-substituted
   833	/// point is visible instead of asking about the unsubstituted authored terms.
   834	fn point_read_is_visible(schema: &Schema, read: &crate::ir::Read) -> bool {
   835	    let Some(table) = schema.table(&read.table) else { return false };
   836	    let Some(index) = table.index(&read.index) else { return false };
   837	    if !index.is_plain() || index.components.len() != read.key.len() { return false; }

   839	        (part.column().is_some_and(|column| column == name)).then_some(value));

   846	        (RuleTerm::Literal(value), Expr::Literal(actual)) => value == actual,
   847	        (RuleTerm::Field(field), expression) => pins.iter().find_map(|(name, value)|
   848	            (name.as_str() == field).then_some(*value)).is_some_and(|value| value == expression),
   849	        _ => false,
   850	    }

   853	/// A witness supplies one conjunct; every other conjunct must already hold
   854	/// by the lane's pins. Distribution does not drop a status/role condition.
   855	fn membership_rule(rule: &RuleExpr, witness: &impl Fn(&RuleExpr) -> bool, pinned: &impl Fn(&RuleExpr) -> bool) -> bool {
   856	    match rule {
   857	        RuleExpr::Or(parts) => parts.iter().any(|part| membership_rule(part, witness, pinned)),
   858	        RuleExpr::And(parts) => parts.iter().enumerate().any(|(at, part)|
   859	            membership_rule(part, witness, pinned) && parts.iter().enumerate().all(|(other, rule)| at == other || pinned(rule))),
   860	        _ => witness(rule),
   861	    }
   862	}

   869	fn required_source_equality<'a>(filter: &'a Expr, column: &str) -> Option<&'a Expr> {
   870	    match filter {
   871	        Expr::Binary { op: crate::ir::BinaryOp::And, left, right } =>
   872	            required_source_equality(left, column).or_else(|| required_source_equality(right, column)),

    42	pub(super) fn membership_physical_issue(module: &LeanModule, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<&'static str> {
    43	    if read.range.is_none() || !read.keys.is_empty() { return None }
    44	    let target = module.tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
    45	    let decl = module.surface_tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
    46	    let rule = decl.rules.iter().find(|rule| rule.effect == Effect::Read && rule.bindings.is_empty()).map(|rule| &rule.value)?;
    47	    let mut leaves = Vec::new();
    48	    flatten_or(rule, &mut leaves);
    49	    let mut parties = Vec::new();
    50	    let mut memberships = Vec::new();
    51	    for leaf in leaves {
    52	        if let Some(column) = party_column(target, leaf) { parties.push(column.to_owned()); }
    53	        else if let Some(membership) = membership_physical(module, target, leaf) { memberships.push(membership); }
    54	        else { return None; }
    55	    }
    56	    if memberships.is_empty() { return None }
    57	    if memberships.iter().any(|membership| !membership.source_admissible) {
    58	        return Some("page-membership-source-unique");
    59	    }
    60	    let mut fits = Vec::new();
    61	    for membership in &memberships {
    62	        let Some(table) = module.tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == membership.source) else { return Some("page-membership-key-bound") };
    63	        fits.push(match &membership.index {
    64	            Some(index) => model_selected_index_fit(module, table, index),
    65	            None => model_plain_index_fit(module, table, &membership.columns, false),
    66	        });
    67	    }
    68	    let (system_pins, branches) = model_page_pins(target, read);
    69	    fits.push(model_plain_index_fit(module, target, &model_lane_columns(system_pins, &read.order_keys), true));
    70	    for branch in branches {
    71	        for prefix in parties.iter().chain(memberships.iter().map(|membership| &membership.target_parent)) {
    72	            let mut pins = branch.clone();
    73	            if !pins.contains(prefix) { pins.push(prefix.clone()); pins.sort(); }
    74	            fits.push(model_plain_index_fit(module, target, &model_lane_columns(pins, &read.order_keys), true));
    75	        }
    76	    }
    77	    if fits.contains(&ModelIndexFit::Ambiguous) { Some("page-membership-index-selection") }
    78	    else if fits.contains(&ModelIndexFit::Nonportable) { Some("page-membership-key-bound") }
    79	    else { None }
    80	}

    82	/// Compound PAGE3 remains outside the executable model when its physical

    85	pub(super) fn compound_physical_issue(module: &LeanModule, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<&'static str> {
    86	    if read.range.is_none() || !read.keys.is_empty() { return None; }

    88	    let decl = module.surface_tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
    89	    let rule = decl.rules.iter().find(|rule| rule.effect == Effect::Read)?.value.clone();
    90	    fn compound(e: &Expr) -> bool {
    91	        match e {
    92	            Expr::Binary { op, .. } if op == "and" => true,
    93	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => compound(left) || compound(right),
    94	            _ => false,
    95	        }
    96	    }

   100	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => {
   101	                let mut out = ranges(left)?; out.extend(ranges(right)?); out
   102	            }
   103	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "and" => {

   114	    // their where alternatives already have an independent physical proof.

   133	        }).collect();

   149	                    pins.push(membership.target_parent);

   167	/// The model's direct grant theorem remains positive. Standard bounded
   168	/// negative-grant pages use the independent branch classifier; direct scalar
   169	/// absence/aggregation and fallible-filter fallback stay explicitly outside
   170	/// that executable completeness fragment until its negative-view proof lands.
   171	pub(super) fn negative_grant_issue(module: &LeanModule, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<&'static str> {
   172	    if !read.keys.is_empty() && read.filter.is_none() && read.range.is_none() { return None; }
   173	    fn negative_probe(expr: &Expr) -> bool {
   174	        match expr {
   175	            Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } if op=="not" => matches!(value.as_ref(),Expr::Exists {..}),
   176	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op=="and" || op=="or" => negative_probe(left) || negative_probe(right),
   177	            _=>false,
   178	        }
   179	    }
   180	    let table = module.surface_tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
   181	    let rule = &table.rules.iter().find(|rule| rule.effect == Effect::Read)?.value;
   182	    if !negative_probe(rule) { return None; }
   183	    fn total(expr: &Expr) -> bool {
   184	        match expr {
   185	            Expr::Name { .. } | Expr::Literal { .. } | Expr::Focal { .. } => true,
   186	            Expr::Member { base, .. } => matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Focal { .. }),
   187	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } => matches!(op.as_str(), "=" | "!=" | "<" | "<=" | ">" | ">=" | "and" | "or" | "in") && total(left) && total(right),
   188	            Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } => op == "not" && total(value),
   189	            Expr::Array { items, .. } => items.iter().all(total), _ => false,
   190	        }
   191	    }
   192	    fn distributed_width(expr: &Expr) -> usize {
   193	        match expr {
   194	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op=="or" => distributed_width(left).saturating_add(distributed_width(right)).min(8),
   195	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op=="and" => distributed_width(left).saturating_mul(distributed_width(right)).min(8),
   196	            _=>1,
   197	        }
   198	    }
   199	    (read.range.is_none() || read.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(|filter| !total(filter)) || distributed_width(rule) >= 8)
   200	        .then_some("page-negative-grant-fallback")
   201	}

   203	/// Named order normalization is speculative in the native compiler. Only
   204	/// the independently modeled unfiltered mixed range fragment normalizes here;
   205	/// filtered or one-party fallback candidates stay named Unsupported.
   206	pub(super) fn named_merge_fragment(module: &LeanModule, read: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
   207	    if read.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(|filter| !matches!(filter, Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Bool(true), .. })) { return false; }
   208	    let Some(target)=module.tables.iter().find(|t|t.name==read.table) else {return false;};
   209	    let Some(rule)=module.surface_tables.iter().find(|t|t.name==read.table).and_then(|t|t.rules.iter().find(|r|r.effect==Effect::Read)).map(|r|&r.value) else {return false;};
   210	    if !matches!(rule,Expr::Binary {op,..} if op=="or") {return false;}
   211	    let mut leaves=Vec::new();flatten_or(rule,&mut leaves);
   212	    leaves.len()<=7 && leaves.iter().any(|leaf|party_column(target,leaf).is_some())

   214	            || model_equality_column(target,leaf).is_some())
   215	}

     1	//! A query page whose predicate, or whose table's read rule, is a
     2	//! disjunction of branches each pinning an index prefix that decides the read
     3	//! rule plans as the merge of the branch ranges (LLP 3000.036.001): `tasks where
     4	//! .owner = viewer or .assignee = viewer last 50 by .at after c`, or the same
     5	//! list read through `read <- .owner = viewer or .assignee = viewer`. A page
     6	//! its own pins already decide is never rewritten, so no complete plan, index
     7	//! or cursor changes.
     8	use super::{catalog::TableInfo, intent::{self, Requests}};
     9	use crate::ast::*;
    10	use crate::diag::Site;
    11	use snapback4_core::ir::{BRANCH_LANE, GRANT_LANE, MEMBERSHIP_LANE, SYSTEM_LANE};
    12	use std::collections::BTreeMap;
    13	
    14	/// At most this many branches: each is an index range read ahead by one
    15	/// page, and each may be an index the planner synthesizes.
    16	pub(super) const MAX_BRANCHES: usize = 8;
    17	
    18	type Tables = BTreeMap<String, TableInfo>;
    19	
    20	/// One branch's conjuncts, and whether it is the grant branch.
    21	type Branch = (Vec<Expr>, bool);

    23	/// Only turn an implicit whole-group read into a page when the original
    24	/// prefix has a stored bound. A party cap does not bound a staff's all-row
    25	/// branch. The original bound applies to the union, rather than each party.
    26	fn bounded_page(tables: &Tables, requests: &Requests, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<ReadExpr> {
    27	    if read.range.is_some() { return Some(read.clone()); }
    28	    let table = tables.get(&read.table)?;
    29	    let mut planned = read.clone();
    30	    let mut probe = requests.clone();
    31	    probe.read(tables, &mut planned, false);
    32	    let pinned: Vec<String> = planned.keys.iter().filter_map(|key| key.column.clone()).collect();
    33	    let cap = table.decl.caps.iter().filter(|cap| cap.by.iter().all(|column| pinned.contains(column))
    34	        && !cap.by.iter().any(|column| table.decl.columns.iter().any(|field| &field.name == column
    35	            && field.on_delete.as_ref().is_some_and(|(action, _)| action == &OnDelete::SetNull))))
    36	        .map(|cap| cap.max).chain(table.indexes.iter().filter(|index| table.total_unique(index))
    37	            .filter(|index| index.columns.iter().all(|column| pinned.contains(column))).map(|_| 1)).min()?;
    38	    if u64::from(cap) > snapback4_core::Ceilings::default().examined { return None; }
    39	    let mut page = read.clone();
    40	    page.range = Some(RangeSpec { order: Order::Asc, take: cap, cursor: None, site: read.site.clone() });
    41	    Some(page)
    42	}

    44	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#2 — the rule: where-branches first, then the read rule's parties and readable grants.
    45	pub(super) fn expand(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &std::collections::BTreeSet<(String, String)>, requests: &mut Requests, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<MergeExpr> {
    46	    // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#16 — implicit whole-group reads retain their proven schema bound.
    47	    let mut authored = read.clone();
    48	    // A named plain range states the same column order. Normalize only a
    49	    // speculative merge input; a declined candidate retains the authored read.
    50	    if authored.range.is_some() && authored.keys.is_empty() && authored.matching.is_none()
    51	        && authored.computed.is_empty() && authored.cursor_prefix.is_none()
    52	        && let Some(index) = authored.index.as_ref().and_then(|name| tables.get(&authored.table)?.indexes.iter().find(|index| &index.name == name))
    53	        && index.is_plain() {
    54	        authored.order_keys = index.columns.clone(); authored.index = None;
    55	    }
    56	    let normalized = bounded_page(tables, requests, &authored)?;
    57	    let read = &normalized;
    58	    // Vacancy is an exact negative lane, rather than a generic positive
    59	    // grant branch. Classify it before the positive branch planner.
    60	    // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — exact vacancy retains a full private scan.
    61	    if let Some(merge) = super::intent_compound::vacancy(tables, synthesized, requests, read) { return Some(merge); }
    62	    if let Some(merge) = scoped_membership(tables, synthesized, requests, read) { return Some(merge); }
    63	    if let Some(merge) = membership(tables, synthesized, requests, read) { return Some(merge); }
    64	    // An exact owner-or-membership rule belongs wholly to PAGE. If one of its
    65	    // stricter eligibility checks declines, keep the original scan instead of
    66	    // reinterpreting its probe as a generic grant branch.
    67	    if owner_membership_shape(tables, synthesized, read) && !pinned_memberships(tables, synthesized, read) { return None; }
    68	    let range = read.range.as_ref()?;
    69	    let Some(branches) = branches(tables, synthesized, requests, read, true) else {
    70	        return super::intent_compound::expand(tables, synthesized, requests, read);
    71	    };
    72	    let mut candidate = requests.clone();
    73	    let errors = candidate.error_count();
    74	    let mut lanes = Vec::with_capacity(branches.len());
    75	    let mut order: Option<Vec<String>> = None;
    76	    for (conjuncts, grant) in branches {
    77	        let mut lane = read.clone();
    78	        lane.filter = intent::conjunction(conjuncts);

   253	fn pinned_memberships(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &std::collections::BTreeSet<(String,String)>, read: &ReadExpr) -> bool {

   282	/// Confirm, while requests still live in the throwaway candidate, that every
   283	/// fixed probe component survived source planning as either a prefix pin or
   284	/// an exact necessary residual equality. The core repeats this check on bound
   285	/// IR; keeping it here prevents a later fallback from retaining unused indexes.
   286	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#14 — membership candidates commit only after their complete witness is visible in discovery.
   287	fn membership_source_witness(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &std::collections::BTreeSet<(String, String)>,
   288	    focal: &TableInfo, predicate: &Expr, source: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
   289	    let Expr::Exists { read: probe, .. } = predicate else { return false };
   290	    let Some(columns) = super::intent_lanes::probe_columns(tables, synthesized, focal, probe) else { return false };
   291	    probe.keys.iter().zip(columns).all(|(key, column)| intent::column(&key.value).is_some()
   292	        || source_fixed(source, &column, &key.value))
   293	}
   294	
   295	fn source_fixed(source: &ReadExpr, column: &str, value: &Expr) -> bool {
   296	    source.keys.iter().any(|key| key.column.as_deref() == Some(column) && same_fixed(&key.value, value))
   297	        || source.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(|filter| required_source_equality(filter, column, value))
   298	}
   299	
   300	fn required_source_equality(filter: &Expr, column: &str, value: &Expr) -> bool {
   301	    match filter {
   302	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "and" =>
   303	            required_source_equality(left, column, value) || required_source_equality(right, column, value),
   304	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "=" && intent::column(left) == Some(column) => same_fixed(right, value),
   305	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "=" && intent::column(right) == Some(column) => same_fixed(left, value),
   306	        _ => false,
   307	    }
   308	}
   309	
   310	fn same_fixed(left: &Expr, right: &Expr) -> bool {
   311	    match (left, right) {
   312	        (Expr::Name { name: a, .. }, Expr::Name { name: b, .. }) => a == b,
   313	        (Expr::Literal { value: a, .. }, Expr::Literal { value: b, .. }) => a == b,
   314	        (Expr::Unary { op: a, value: av, .. }, Expr::Unary { op: b, value: bv, .. }) => a == b && same_fixed(av, bv),
   315	        _ => false,
   316	    }
   317	}

   319	pub(super) fn push_membership_lane(candidate: &Requests, table: &TableInfo, lanes: &mut Vec<MergeLane>, order: &mut Option<Vec<String>>, lane: ReadExpr,
   320	    branch: &str, loop_: Option<(String, Expr)>, distinct: Option<Expr>, admission: Option<Box<Expr>>) -> Option<()> {
   321	    if order.get_or_insert_with(|| lane.order_keys.clone()) != &lane.order_keys || lane.index.is_none() { return None; }
   322	    if lane.keys.len() + lane.order_keys.len() > 8 || !candidate.branch_index_fits(table, &lane) { return None; }
   323	    lanes.push(MergeLane { admission, nulls: None, distinct, branch: Some(branch.into()), planned: Some(Box::new(lane)), project: None,
   324	        table: None, prefix: Vec::new(), columns: None, loop_, site: table.decl.site.clone() }); Some(())
   325	}
   326	
   327	fn membership_source_capped(tables: &Tables, source: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
   328	    let Some(table) = tables.get(&source.table) else { return false };
   329	    let pinned: Vec<&str> = source.keys.iter().filter_map(|key| key.column.as_deref()).collect();
   330	    table.decl.caps.iter().any(|cap| cap.by.iter().all(|column| pinned.contains(&column.as_str()))
   331	        && !cap.by.iter().any(|column| table.decl.columns.iter().any(|field| &field.name == column
   332	            && field.on_delete.as_ref().is_some_and(|(action, _)| action == &OnDelete::SetNull))))
   333	}
   334	
   335	fn membership_cap_repair(table: &TableInfo, source: &ReadExpr, index: &IndexDecl) -> Option<String> {
   336	    let fixed = index.columns.iter().zip(&source.keys).filter_map(|(column, key)| {
   337	        let non_null = matches!(&key.value, Expr::Name { name, .. } if name == "viewer")
   338	            || matches!(&key.value, Expr::Literal { value, .. } if !matches!(value, Literal::Null))
   339	            || matches!(&key.value, Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "-" && matches!(value.as_ref(), Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Number(_), .. }));
   340	        (column != "id" && non_null && table.columns.get(column).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.optional)).then_some(column.clone())
